SOURCE DATA repo://abiogenesis/specification/requirements/product/REQ-P-SELF-CONFORMANCE.md sha256:4fa6ea8b7d655a395916b135e333e5f96a5bb309c9166c3e41d14c67d2e21f61
"# REQ-P-SELF-CONFORMANCE - ABIogenesis Product Self-Conformance\n\n**Status**: Active - T-283 base; exact Definition-selected STDO qualification basis\n**Category**: Constraint / Verification\n**Date**: 2026-07-25\n**Derives from**: [INTENT.md](../../INTENT.md) INT-001,\n[PRODUCT.md](../../PRODUCT.md),\n[REQ-M-GTL3-CAPABILITY.md](../mapping/REQ-M-GTL3-CAPABILITY.md),\n[REQ-P-QUAL.md](REQ-P-QUAL.md)\n**Wave**: ABG 5.0\n\n---\n\n## Purpose\n\nDefine the product law by which ABIogenesis applies its published method,\ncontract, conformance, proof, and release rules to its own frozen source and\ncandidate-product subject through the same `ExactCandidateQualification<K>`\nfamily used by release qualification. The product has no exemption and no\nticket, reviewer, release adapter, or snapshot may become a second conformance\nauthority.\n\n## Subject And Basis\n\n**REQ-P-SELF-CONFORMANCE-001**: Each self-conformance run shall bind one exact\n`ExactCandidateQualification<basis>` projection and its subordinate\n`QualificationLawBasis`. The subject shall identify its closed qualification\nkind, exact source and artifact content, toolchain manifest, installed-product\nand workspace-binding truth when applicable, tenant-conformance manifest, and\nsource-grounded behavioral coverage catalog. The law basis shall identify the specification-method\nversion, applicable rule-catalog version, exact source references, and content\ndigests used to decide the run. ABIogenesis 5.0 release qualification shall\nbind the exact tapped and installed STDO release identity selected by\n`stdo_abiogenesis.json`,\ninstalled-manifest digest, and standards member-set digest.\n\n**REQ-P-SELF-CONFORMANCE-001A**: Missing, stale, conflicting, unreadable, or\ncross-subject basis truth shall produce a typed non-green result. A release cut,\nrelease snapshot, ticket, prior verdict, or mutable source-tree label shall not\nsubstitute for the exact qualification subject or law basis.\n\n**REQ-P-SELF-CONFORMANCE-002**: The frozen subject inventory shall include every\nABIogenesis constitutional, design, realization, proof, ticket/execution-\ncontract, public-seam or exported-contract, product/installer manifest,\nqualification, and release-claim surface applicable to the candidate. An\nunclassified or unreadable required surface shall be a typed incomplete result,\nnot an implicit pass.\n\n**REQ-P-SELF-CONFORMANCE-003**: Applicability shall derive from the published\nmethod, requirement families, tenant-conformance manifest, module ownership,\nand declared public contract identities. File extension, source-tenant path,\nticket status, test name, or reviewer preference shall not create or remove a\nrule obligation.\n\n## Evaluation And Results\n\n**REQ-P-SELF-CONFORMANCE-004**: Self-conformance shall use the existing\nconformance evaluator atom, `AF-22`, under its qualification-bound\n`self_conformance` kind and the published GTL-validator and rule-catalog\npath. It is not another public operation. It shall not create a second method\nchecker, repair engine, release checker, runtime, or hidden manual audit as a\nco-equal source of truth.\n\n**REQ-P-SELF-CONFORMANCE-005**: The result shall carry the exact qualification\nsubject-basis and `QualificationLawBasis` identities and digests, inventory\ndigest, rule applications, typed diagnostics, affected surface refs, evidence\nrefs, and one disposition for each finding and for the whole run. At minimum,\ndispositions shall distinguish passed, failed, inapplicable-with-reason,\nblocked/incomplete, and accepted re-entry work.\n\n**REQ-P-SELF-CONFORMANCE-006**: A waiver, exclusion, or inapplicable\ndisposition shall name the governing authority and reason. Product identity,\nrelease pressure, ticket closure, prior review, or historical acceptance shall\nnot waive an applicable rule.\n\n**REQ-P-SELF-CONFORMANCE-007**: Findings shall remain diagnostics and admitted\nwork pressure. The self-conformance evaluator shall not silently edit\nconstitutional, design, code, proof, ticket, public-contract, or release\nsurfaces. Repair re-enters through the owning change class and ticket or\nexecution contract.\n\n**REQ-P-SELF-CONFORMANCE-007A**: The admitted complete self-conformance result\nshall conserve exact qualification subject/law, source-grounded behavioral\ncoverage, actual execution evidence and independent applicability/sufficiency\njudgment. It is the assessment input to the sole AF22 verdict; it shall not\nemit or reinterpret release qualification itself. Shared evidence is permitted\nwithout a distinct Result per behavior, but missing required coverage remains\nblocked.\n\n## Proof And Exact-Cut Gate\n\n**REQ-P-SELF-CONFORMANCE-008**: Qualification of a `pre_rc_candidate` shall run\nthe self-conformance contract against the real complete ABIogenesis candidate\ntree bound by its exact `ExactCandidateQualification<basis>` projection.\nQualification of an `installed_rc` shall rerun every self-conformance gate made\napplicable by that exact installed subject. A qualifying Product or\nrelease-claim byte change requires a higher RC and its applicable gates, not a\nthird qualification subject at acceptance. A run is green only when every\napplicable required surface and rule has a typed terminal disposition.\n\n**REQ-P-SELF-CONFORMANCE-009**: A seeded-negative matrix shall introduce\nrepresentative missing authority, broken traceability, unowned public contract,\ndesign/code drift, malformed proof claim, ticket/closure mismatch, and\nrelease-identity mismatch defects. Each seed shall produce its expected stable\ndiagnostic and non-green disposition.\n\n**REQ-P-SELF-CONFORMANCE-010**: The release-grade self-conformance result shall\nbind the exact ABIogenesis 5.0 qualification subject, its tenant-conformance\nmanifest, frozen subject inventory, and exact method/rule/source basis. A result\nover different bytes or a different law basis, an incomplete inventory, an\nunresolvable basis, or a seeded defect that passes shall refuse complete self-conformance\nand therefore qualification.\n\n**REQ-P-SELF-CONFORMANCE-011**: The bounded A5-R1 release snapshot manifest may\ncite the exact self-conformance result and the qualification verdict that\nauthorized its cut. The snapshot is output evidence only. It shall not qualify\nits own input, reinterpret findings, replace their dispositions, or turn a red,\nblocked, incomplete, stale, cross-basis, or bypassed result green.\n\n## Bounded Scope\n\n**REQ-P-SELF-CONFORMANCE-012**: ABIogenesis 5.0 self-conformance is a bounded\nexact-product gate. It does not claim a universal autonomous specification\nrepair product, hosted policy service, general repository auditor, new\nconstitutional ontology, or substitute for ordinary F_H decisions and lawful\nrepricing.\n"

SOURCE DATA repo://abiogenesis/specification/requirements/product/REQ-P-QUAL.md sha256:9ec53d8053fcae9780ad0d161c0b3fe605523319965b59d5653865254bac1831
"# REQ-P-QUAL — Qualification Infrastructure\n\n**Status**: Active - T-283 base; exact Definition-selected STDO qualification basis\n**Category**: Verification\n**Date**: 2026-07-25\n**Updated**: 2026-09-28\n**Derives from**: INT-001 (installed product and release qualification), INT-005 (run governance, failure classification), [PRODUCT.md](../../PRODUCT.md), [SPEC_METHOD.md](stdo://releases/v2.5.1-rc.2/standards/SPEC_METHOD.md) (Verification Layers)\n**Wave**: ABG 5.0\n\n---\n\n## Purpose\n\nThe test and qualification infrastructure is a product surface with its own correctness requirements. Without formal requirements, failure modes are diagnosed ad-hoc, the same defects recur, and lineage cannot be established from intent through to test evidence.\n\nThis requirement establishes the constitutional rules for the qualification infrastructure itself — transport readiness, failure mode separation, artifact integrity, timeout contracts, and forensic evidence. Every test that validates product behavior must itself satisfy these requirements.\n\nGate applicability follows [Product Release Boundaries](../../PRODUCT.md#50-and-51-release-boundaries).\nStandalone Consensus, mandatory host parity, native human response/resume and\nthe dedicated F12/S04 observer/tuner Product are 5.1 qualification claims. Their\ndeferral does not waive retained local traversal, truthful blocking, runtime\nliveness, complete selected F17/S06 outcomes or actual human RC acceptance.\nThe selected ordinary governance/default-library discriminator follows\n`REQ-P-SCENARIOS-018` within existing S02/S03/S06, not an additional campaign.\n\n---\n\n## Transport Readiness\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-001**: An agent skip gate shall verify that the agent is **ready to serve requests**, not merely present on PATH. Readiness means the agent can accept a prompt and return a coherent response within a bounded probe timeout.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-002**: When a readiness probe fails, the test shall be **skipped with a diagnostic reason**, not failed. \"Agent not ready\" is an environment precondition, not a test failure.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-003**: The readiness probe shall be the **cheapest reliable check** — a trivial prompt with a deterministic expected response. It shall not perform substantive work or consume significant resources.\n\n---\n\n## Failure Mode Separation\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-004**: Each failure class in the INT-005 taxonomy (`transport_failure`, `no_output`, `contract_failure`, `certification_failure`) shall produce a **distinct, diagnosable signal** in test output. A test observer shall be able to determine the failure class from the test output alone, without reading source code.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-005**: Transport failures (nonzero exit, timeout, crash) shall remain **diagnosable transport truth**. They shall not be masked as artifact quality failures or silently converted to empty output. A preserved artifact may only rescue the turn when ABG deterministically validates it as the authoritative result for that boundary; mere artifact presence is insufficient.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-006**: The actual selected transport shall preserve nonzero exit,\ncrash and timeout as failure with attributable stdout/stderr and process truth;\nfailed output shall not be admitted as success merely because bytes exist.\n\n---\n\n## Artifact Integrity\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-007**: If the agent did not write the expected artifact file, the test infrastructure shall raise an **artifact-missing error** with diagnostic context (raw response preview, expected path). It shall not substitute the agent's stdout as the artifact.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-008**: The deterministic judge shall only evaluate artifacts that were **written by the agent through the tool surface**, not artifacts synthesized from transport output by the test harness. Fallback masking converts transport/readiness failures into artifact-quality failures, making diagnosis impossible.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-009**: When an artifact exists but is below a minimum viable size (e.g., placeholder content), it shall be treated as **no_output**, not as a valid artifact to judge.\n\n---\n\n## Timeout Contracts\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-010**: Selected test, command, native actor and caller supervision\nbounds shall be explicit and proportional to the declared ask, with each\napplicable layer identified. No particular test runner or historical default\nis mandated.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-011**: Enclosing supervision shall allow the selected lower owner\nto report its terminal result and preserve failure evidence. A deadline or\nprocess termination shall identify the layer and bound; it shall not be\nreported as a completed test or successful qualification.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-012**: When a transport timeout fires, the resulting error shall **name the timeout value and the agent** so the operator can distinguish \"agent was slow\" from \"transport configuration too aggressive.\"\n\n---\n\n## Qualification Lanes\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-013**: Smoke tests and qualification tests shall be **structurally distinct test classes** with different assertion contracts:\n  - Smoke: asserts the protocol completed (response exists, judge ran). Does not assert artifact quality.\n  - Qualification: asserts the judge passed. This is the release gate.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-014**: A qualification question that claims statistical\nconfidence shall declare and parameterize its independent run count. The count\nshall be a named constant rather than a magic number, and the resulting claim\nshall state the population it supports. A bounded deterministic, installed, or\nlive steel-thread gate may require one declared run when it claims only that\nthe exact scenario completed; ABG 5.0 does not claim generic pass@k\ncharacterization.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-015**: Entropy campaign tests (sequential dispatches in a shared sandbox, delta trend over accumulated state) shall be a **separate lane** from fresh-sandbox qualification. Entropy campaigns require artifact integrity (REQ-P-QUAL-007) to be satisfied — no fallback masking.\n\n---\n\n## Forensic Evidence\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-016**: Every live qualification run shall **archive** the following artifacts for postmortem:\n  - The manifest JSON (what was dispatched)\n  - The prompt text (what the agent received)\n  - The raw response (what the agent returned)\n  - The artifact content (what was judged)\n  - The judge verdict (pass/fail with per-criterion evidence)\n  - The model identifier and transport method\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-017**: Archive artifacts shall be written **before** the test assertion fires. A failing test that destroys its own evidence is a forensic dead end.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-018**: The archive path shall encode **test identity** (scenario name, run ID, timestamp) so that postmortem can correlate a specific test failure to its evidence without grepping.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-018A**: Every sandbox-backed scenario or qualification run shall support a **persistent run archive** rooted under a stable, non-temporary test-run directory. Pytest temporary workspaces are execution scratch space; they are not sufficient as the postmortem record.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-018B**: A persistent run archive shall preserve the sufficient,\nexact material for postmortem of its selected installed path: source/artifact\nidentities, actual requests and results, relevant worksite changes, native\nprocess/command observations, event resource identity and genuine readback or\nclose receipts where produced. Historical directory names and copying\nirrelevant build caches are not requirements; missing terminal evidence\nremains explicitly missing.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-018C**: A persistent run archive shall also preserve **run metadata and operator-facing summaries**, including at minimum:\n  - a run metadata record (`run.json` or equivalent) containing timestamp, test identity, source commit, interpreter, and invoked commands\n  - a summarized postmortem view (`summary.json` or equivalent) containing convergence state, failing evaluators, and key artifact references\n  - captured subprocess stdout/stderr where subprocess transport or CLI orchestration is involved\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-018D**: Persistent run archives shall be **immutable and non-overwriting**. Each run receives a new path keyed by scenario/use-case identity plus sortable timestamp; a later run shall not destroy or mutate a prior archive.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-018E**: Archive materialization shall occur **before** sandbox cleanup or test fixture teardown. If execution uses temporary directories, the archive copy must be completed before those directories are eligible for deletion.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-018F**: The archive structure shall be **scenario-oriented**, not merely test-run-oriented. A postmortem operator shall be able to navigate by use case first, then timestamped run, without requiring pytest internals or temporary-directory names.\n\n---\n\n## Installed Sandbox Population\n\nThe installed substrate contract is governed by\n[REQ-P-INSTALL.md](./REQ-P-INSTALL.md). Qualification requirements here define\nthe proving obligations over that product contract.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-018G**: Any sandbox-backed downstream qualification that claims installed ABG substrate truth shall populate the sandbox through a public ABG installer surface. Source-tree imports, private test helpers, or harness-only package bindings are not sufficient closure evidence for installed runtime behavior.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-018H**: A public ABG installer shall write inspectable installed-runtime truth that includes package identity, installer manifest path, install manifest path, command binding paths, runtime identity, event/projection evidence roots, and the installed package root used by downstream execution.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-018I**: Installer qualification shall preserve a persistent archive/postmortem under the stable test-run archive root. The archive shall include install manifest evidence, installer manifest evidence, package identity, command binding evidence, runtime identity, event/projection evidence, and an operator-facing postmortem summary.\n\n---\n\n## Diff-Execution Witness Gate\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-025**: Approval shall include attributable execution evidence for\nthe changed behavior and its affected owning boundaries, including meaningful\nnearest-negative cases. Select the proof level from the supported claim:\ncomponent checks do not establish installed outcomes, and export enumeration\nor blanket coverage of every never-committed file does not establish behavior.\nStill-valid exact-subject evidence may be reused with its scope and identity;\nunexercised material changes remain explicit limitations.\n\n## Installed Proof\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-026**: Any qualification claim of installed Product behavior shall\nexecute through the packed-and-installed ordinary surface. Source component\nchecks retain their narrower claims. Historical test-file exemption counts,\nprivate legacy APIs and a particular migration ticket do not define release\nqualification or excuse missing current installed evidence.\n\n---\n\n## Release Snapshot Bundles\n\nRelease snapshot bundles are release-process output artifacts. They record one\nalready-qualified published RC cut; later acceptance reports that same cut.\nThey do not make a version identifier live constitutional project truth or\nbecome an input to the qualification that authorized their creation.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-050**: `release.snapshot` / `AF-25` shall materialize each\npackage-first RC as one immutable, versioned cut and snapshot bundle only after\nadmission of the exact same-subject and same-law-basis green, non-bypassed\n`ExactCandidateQualification<verdict>` projection for its `pre_rc_candidate`.\nIts `tapped_release` member shall report qualification and actual human\nacceptance of that same unchanged RC, not materialize another cut or snapshot.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-051**: The snapshot bundle shall include, at minimum, the package\ntarball, release snapshot manifest, checksum file, release note copy when one\nexists, package identity, source ref, source commit, build command, pack command,\nand verification facts.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-052**: The release snapshot manifest shall be the authoritative\nread model for the snapshot artifact set produced by its cut. Stray tenant-root\ntarballs, dry-run pack output, release-note prose, or the snapshot itself shall\nnot substitute for the qualification basis, authenticated assessment results, or verdict\nthat authorized the cut.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-053**: Snapshot creation shall fail closed when the source tree is\ndirty, when the package identity does not match the requested release identity,\nor when the target versioned snapshot root already contains files.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-054**: Snapshot creation shall compute and record deterministic\nchecksums for the package tarball and generated release snapshot files.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-055**: Release snapshot tooling shall package an explicit source\nroot/ref and shall not silently reinterpret the mutable development checkout as\nan already tagged release cut.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-056**: The release snapshot shall be **self-certifying as a bounded\nread model**, not as a second evaluator. Snapshot creation embeds the exact\nqualification subject and law-basis refs, complete assessment and execution-evidence citations,\ngreen non-bypassed verdict, build/lint/test outcomes, and parsed test summary in\nthe release snapshot manifest. It shall not execute or reinterpret those gates.\nA red, incomplete, stale, cross-basis, or bypassed result refuses a\nRELEASE-GRADE cut. Declared bypass booleans may be honored only for an explicitly\nnon-release snapshot; a bypassed gate records null evidence and shall never be\nrepresented as green or promotable.\n\nGap: the current release-snapshot request still accepts build, lint, and test\nbypass booleans for a release-grade request. T-247 shall make release-grade\nadmission reject every mandatory-gate or exact-cut bypass and pin the red and\nbypassed differentials before the ABIogenesis 5.0 exact-candidate gate freezes\nits qualification input.\n\n## ABIogenesis 5.0 Exact-Cut Qualification\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-057**: ABIogenesis 5.0 qualification shall use one generic\n`ExactCandidateQualification<K>` contract family whose closed subject kinds are\n`pre_rc_candidate | installed_rc`. The family shall\npublish one immutable, content-addressed `basis` projection and one typed\n`verdict` projection for each exact subject. It shall not create separately\nauthored qualification models for those subjects. Acceptance of the qualified\nunchanged RC shall not introduce a third subject kind or final-cut delta.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-057A**: Every `ExactCandidateQualification<basis>` projection shall\nbind its exact subject kind, source and artifact content, toolchain manifest,\ninstalled-product and workspace-binding truth when applicable,\ntenant-conformance manifest, source-grounded coverage catalog, and one subordinate\n`QualificationLawBasis`.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-057B**: `QualificationLawBasis` shall bind the exact tapped and\ninstalled STDO release identity selected by `stdo_abiogenesis.json`, installed-manifest digest,\nstandards member-set digest, method version, rule-catalog version, source refs,\nand content digests used by the owning gates.\nIts identity and digest shall be preserved unchanged through every admitted\nauthenticated assessment result, the coverage catalog, the declared\n`C.of(AF-22)` argument, and the resulting\n`ExactCandidateQualification<verdict>` projection.\n\n**REQ-P-QUAL-057C**: The `ExactCandidateQualification<basis>` projection for an\nABIogenesis 5.0 `pre_rc_candidate` shall bind the prospective published-RC\nidentity and version, the exact installed candidate identity, content and\ninstall-artifact digests, product-manifest digest, workspace binding, and\ntenant-conformance manifest identity and digest. The exact candidate artifact\nbytes shall already carry that prospective published-RC identity.\nSource-tree execution or evidence from different candidate content shall not\nsatisfy the `pre_rc_candidate` gate. Its prospective RC namespace, declared\nprofile, Project Subtree, version line and positive ordinal, exact Product\ninventory and release-claim bytes shall be fixed before qualification. Actual\ntag-object and publication-receipt identities are outputs of publication, not\nfabricated prequalification inputs. typeof x === \"object\" && !Array.isArray(x);\nconst one = <T>(xs: readonly T[]): T | null => xs.length === 1 ? xs[0]! : null;\nconst coord = (ref: string, digest: string) => ({ ref, digest: digest as `sha256:${string}` });\nfunction declarationsOf(input: unknown, resources?: QualificationResources): readonly AbgHistoricalDeclarationProof[] {\n  if (!record(input)) return [];\n  if (input.kind === referenceDependencies(input: unknown, resources?: QualificationResources): boolean {\n  try {\n    if (!record(input)) return true;\n    if (isQualificationAssessmentInput(input) && input) || !record(input.resource)) return false; resources.manifest(input.resource as unknown as QualificationCoordinate); }\n    return true;\n  } catch { return false; }\n}\nfunction ownedProof(proof: QualificationProofResource, resources?: QualificationResources): QualificationEmbeddedProofResource {\n  const facts = executionProofFacts.get(proof);\n  const selectedResources = They are\n// discarded with its owner and never authorize a caller's claimed coordinate.\nconst NATIVE_PROOF = Symbol(\"qualification_native_proof\");\ntype GraphOwner = NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof deriveGraphOwner>>;\nclass NativeProofDerivation {\n  readonly graphs = new Map<string, GraphOwner>();\n  readonly executionSources = new Map<string, { declaration: NonNullable<QualificationProofResource[\"executionSources\"]>[number];\n    proof: QualificationProofResource }>();\n  readonly declarations = new Map<string, { proof: AbgHistoricalDeclarationProof;\n    environment: Parameters<typeof reconstructHistoricalDeclarationCatalog>[1];\n    value: ReturnType<typeof reconstructHistoricalDeclarationCatalog> }>();\n  readonly states = new Map<string, { result: RuntimeEvent; judgment: RuntimeEvent; value: RehydratedAdmittedCCallState }>();\n  readonly terminals = new Map<string, { rows: readonly RuntimeEvent[];\n    terminal: ReturnType<typeof GraphCallProjectionPort.graph_call_result>; replay: ReturnType<typeof projectRunTruthAtDurablePrefix> }>();\n  readonly assessments = new Map<string, { input: NativeRuntimeAssessmentInput; value: Readonly<NativeRuntimeAssessment> }>();\n  readonly verdicts = new Map<string, { input: QualificationVerdictInput; value: Readonly<ExactCandidateQualification<\"verdict\">> }>();\n}\nconst nativeFacts = (prefix: ReturnType<typeof selectValidatedRuntimeEventPrefix>) =>\n  runtimePrefixComputation(prefix, NATIVE_PROOF, () => new NativeProofDerivation());\n// Equality is only a positive reuse discriminator for an already authenticated\n// immutable fact. The canonical relation remains the fallback for all data.\nconst sameFact = (a: unknown, b: unknown) => a === b || isDeepStrictEqual(a, b) || same(a, b);\n// One operation borrows each authenticated source prefix and workspace\n// projection. These private associations cannot be supplied in JSON or confer\n// authority on copied coordinates. The existing cold owners still acquire it.\ntype ExecutionSourceFacts = { events: readonly RuntimeEvent[];\n  artifactTruth?: ExactPrefixArtifactTruthProjection;\n  environments: Map<string, ReturnType<typeof projectExactPrefixWorkspaceEnvironment>>; resources?: QualificationResources | undefined };\nconst executionProofFacts = new WeakMap<QualificationProofResource, ExecutionSourceFacts>();\nfunction acquireExecutionSource(coordinate: DurablePrefixCoordinate): ExecutionSourceFacts | null {\n  const artifactTruth = projectExactPrefixArtifactTruth(coordinate);\n  if (artifactTruth.kind !== \"exact_prefix_artifact_truth_projection\") return null;\n  const prefix = runtimePrefixFromArtifactTruth(artifactTruth);\n  if (prefix === null) return null;\n  return { events: runtimeEventsFromValidatedPrefix(prefix), artifactTruth, environments: new Map() };\n}\nfunction proofEvents(proof: QualificationProofResource): readonly RuntimeEvent[] {\n  return executionProofFacts.get(proof)?.events ?? readRuntimeEventsAtDurablePrefix(proof.prefix as DurablePrefixCoordinate);\n}\nfunction graphOwner(coordinate: DurablePrefixCoordinate, ref: string, declarations: readonly AbgHistoricalDeclarationProof[], source?: ExecutionSourceFacts) {\n  const events = source?.events ?? readRuntimeEventsAtDurablePrefix(coordinate), prefix = selectValidatedRuntimeEventPrefix(events);\n  const opened = one(events.filter(e => e.kind === \"c_call_opened\" && e.aggregateId === ref));\n  if (opened === null) return null;\n  const facts = nativeFacts(prefix), known = facts.graphs.get(ref);\n  if (known !== undefined && referenceDependencies(known.root.rawInputValue, source?.resources) === false) return null;\n  const selectedProofs = known === undefined ? [] : (declarations.length === 0 ? [known.proof] :\n    declarations.filter(d => d.catalog?.basisDigest === known.root.catalogBasisDigest && d.catalogView?.viewDigest === known.root.catalogViewDigest));\n  if (known !== undefined && known.opened === opened && selectedProofs.length === 1 && sameFact(selectedProofs[0], known.proof))\n    return { ...known, events, prefix };\n  const owner = deriveGraphOwner(coordinate, ref, declarations, events, prefix, opened, source);\n  // Retention owns a distinct small wrapper. The returned projection view\n  // remains caller-editable without editing an authenticated retained fact.\n  if (owner !== null) facts.graphs.set(ref, Object.freeze({ ...owner }));\n  return owner;\n}\n/** Reuses the historical Product verifier and native CCall owner. No supplied\n * declaration becomes authority by passing its own structural guard. */\nfunction deriveGraphOwner(prefixCoordinate: DurablePrefixCoordinate, cCallRef: string, declarations: readonly AbgHistoricalDeclarationProof[],\n  events: readonly RuntimeEvent[], prefix: ReturnType<typeof selectValidatedRuntimeEventPrefix>, opened: RuntimeEvent, source?: ExecutionSourceFacts) {\n  const execution = opened?.basisId === undefined ? projectExactPrefixWorkspaceEnvironment(prefixCoordinate, coord(root.workspaceBindingId, root.workspaceBindingDigest))\n      : projectWorkspaceEnvironmentFromArtifactTruth(source.artifactTruth, coord(root.workspaceBindingId, root.workspaceBindingDigest)));\n  source?.environments.set(environmentKey, environment);\n  if (environment.kind !== \"exact_prefix_workspace_environment\") return null;\n  const invocationEvent = one(events.filter(e => e.eventId === invocation.admissionEventRef));\n  if (invocationEvent === null || [...environment.productInstalls.map(i => i.admissionEventRef), environment.workspaceBinding.admissionEventRef].some(ref =>\n    events.filter(e => e.eventId === ref && e.admissionOrdinal < invocationEvent.admissionOrdinal).length !== 1)) return null;\n  if (!referenceDependencies(root.rawInputValue, source?.resources)) return null;\n  const supplied = declarations.length === 0 ? declarationsOf(root.rawInputValue, source?.resources) : declarations;\n  const proofs = supplied.filter(d => d.catalog?.basisDigest === root.catalogBasisDigest && d.catalogView?.viewDigest === root.catalogViewDigest);\n  const proof = one(proofs); if (proof === null) return null;\n  const installs = environment.productInstalls.map(i => projectAdmittedProductInstallByAdmissionEventRef(environment.artifactTruth, i.admissionEventRef));\n  if (installs.some(i => i === null)) return null;\n  const declaredEnvironment = { workspaceBinding: environment.workspaceBindingCandidate,\n    resolvedLock: environment.resolvedProductLock, installedProducts: installs.map(i => i!.candidate) };\n  const facts = nativeFacts(prefix), declarationKey = root.catalogBasisDigest + \":\" + root.catalogViewDigest;\n  const priorDeclaration = facts.declarations.get(declarationKey);\n  const rebuilt = priorDeclaration !== undefined && sameFact(priorDeclaration.proof, proof) &&\n      sameFact(priorDeclaration.environment, declaredEnvironment) ? priorDeclaration.value\n    : reconstructHistoricalDeclarationCatalog(proof, declaredEnvironment);\n  const ownedProof = deepFreeze({ ...proof, catalog: rebuilt.catalog, catalogView: rebuilt.catalogView });\n  facts.declarations.set(declarationKey, { proof: ownedProof, environment: declaredEnvironment, value: rebuilt });\n  const closure = resolveExecutionDeclarationClosure(rebuilt.catalog, rebuilt.catalogView, root.programRef, root.graphFunctionRef);\n  if (closure.kind !== \"resolved_execution_declaration_closure\" ||\n      hash(one(closure.programPublication.programs.filter(p => p.programRef === root.programRef))) !== root.programDigest) return null;\n  const rootContract = selectExactClosureContract(closure, root.resultContractRef);\n  if (rootContract === null || rootContract.contract.contractRef !== invocation.outputContractRef ||\n      hash(rootContract.contract) !== invocation.outputContractDigest || !same(rootContract.owner, invocation.outputContractOwner)) return null;\n  const owner = one(closure.graphFunctionOwners.filter(o => o.declarationRef === execution.graphFunctionRef));\n  if (owner === null) return null;\n  const publication = one(closure.publications.filter(p => p.owningProductId === owner.productId && p.moduleRef === owner.moduleRef &&\n    modulePublicationSemanticDigest(p) === owner.publicationDigest));\n  const fn = publication === null ? null : one(publication.graphFunctions.filter(f => f.name === execution.graphFunctionRef && hash(f) === execution.graphFunctionDigest));\n  if (fn === null || publication === null) return null;\n  const graph = materializeGraph(fn, { invocationAdmissionRef: execution.invocationAdmissionRef, admittedInputRef: execution.rawInputAdmissionRef,\n    admittedInputDigest: execution.rawInputDigest, admittedInput: execution.rawInputValue });\n  if (graph.materializationDigest !== execution.graphDigest || graph.materializationRef !== execution.graphRef) return null;\n  // A workflow's source position comes from its admitted cursor ancestry,\n  // never from the folded Result or a caller-supplied route. The canonical\n  // CCall owner checks that cursor against the exact published GraphFunction.\n  const workflow = record(opened.payload) && opened.payload.callClass === \"workflow\";\n  const sourceInput = workflow ? projectOpenedCCallTraversalInputAtPrefix(prefix, graph, cCallRef) : null;\n  if (workflow && sourceInput === null) return null;\n  const call = projectOpenedCCallCarrierAtPrefix(prefix, graph, cCallRef,\n    sourceInput?.cursor, workflow ? fn : undefined);\n  if (call === null || call.basisId !== execution.basisRef || call.runId !== opened.runId) return null;\n  const set = rehydrateAdmittedImplementationSetAtPrefix(prefix, execution.implementationSetRef);\n  if (set === null || set.implementationSetDigest !== execution.implementationSetDigest) return null;\n  const resolution = call.regime === \"F_H\" ? null : one(set.rows.filter(r => r.graphFunctionRef === call.graphFunctionRef &&\n    r.programLocusRef === call.programLocusRef && r.implementationRef === call.implementationRef &&\n    r.implementationBindingRef === call.implementationBindingRef && r.inputContractRef === call.inputContractRef &&\n    r.outputContractRef === call.outputContractRef && r.computeRegime === call.regime));\n  if (call.callClass === \"leaf\" && call.regime !== \"F_H\" && resolution === null) return null;\n  // Qualification executable GraphFunctions each have one atomic input locus;\n  // an outer workflow passes each child's own admitted raw input. This does not\n  // infer arbitrary same-graph intermediate values from result bytes.\n  const input = execution.rawInputValue;\n  return { events, prefix, opened, execution, root, invocation, environment, closure, publication, graph, call, resolution, input, proof: ownedProof };\n}\nfunction stateAt(owner: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof graphOwner>>): RehydratedAdmittedCCallState | null {\n  const result = one(owner.events.filter(e => e.kind === \"c_call_result_admitted\" && e.aggregateId === owner.call.cCallRef));\n  const judgment = one(owner.events.filter(e => e.kind === \"c_call_judged\" && e.aggregateId === owner.call.cCallRef));\n  if (result === null || judgment === null || !record(result.payload) || !record(judgment.payload)) return null;\n  const facts = nativeFacts(owner.prefix), known = facts.states.get(owner.call.cCallRef);\n  if (known?.result === result && known.judgment === judgment) return known.value;\n  const value = projectAdmittedCCallStateAtPrefix(owner.prefix, owner.call as unknown as Record<string, JsonValue>,\n    { ...result.payload, kind: \"admitted_c_call_result\", schemaVersion: \"5.0.0\", disposition: \"admitted\", admissionEventRef: result.eventId },\n    { ...judgment.payload, kind: \"admitted_c_call_judgment\", schemaVersion: \"5.0.0\", disposition: \"admitted\", admissionEventRef: judgment.eventId });\n  if (value !== null) facts.states.set(owner.call.cCallRef, { result, judgment, value });\n  return value;\n}\n/** Finite declaration count in the selected qualification Program. This is\n * not traversal: no branch, retry, actor or result is executed or inferred. */\nfunction soleDeclaredReducer(owner: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof graphOwner>>): boolean {\n  const functions = owner.closure.publications.flatMap(p => p.graphFunctions);\n  const bindings = owner.closure.publications.flatMap(p => p.implementationBindings);\n  const countGraph = (ref: string, stack: readonly string[]): number => {\n    if (stack.includes(ref)) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;\n    const fn = one(functions.filter(f => f.name === ref));\n    if (fn === null || fn.template.kind !== \"inline_graph\") return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;\n    return fn.template.nodes.reduce((sum, n) => sum + (n.nodeKind === \"c_locus\" ? countTerm(n.term, [...stack, ref]) : 0), 0);\n  };\n  const countTerm = (term: CProgramNode, stack: readonly string[]): number => {\n    switch (term.kind) {\n      case \"c_of\": {\n        const requirement = term.requirement;\n        return term.resultBearing && requirement.kind === \"executable_leaf_requirement\" &&\n          bindings.some(b => b.bindingRef === requirement.implementationBindingRef && b.implementationRef === VERDICT) ? 1 : 0;\n      }\n      case \"c_identity\": return 0;\n      case \"c_workflow\": return countGraph(term.graphFunctionRef, stack);\n      case \"c_compose\": return term.terms.reduce((n, t) => n + countTerm(t, stack), 0);\n      case \"c_batch\": return term.tasks.reduce((n, t) => n + countTerm(t, stack), 0);\n      case \"c_edge\": return [term.transform, term.evaluate, term.consequence].reduce((n, t) => n + countTerm(t, stack), 0);\n      case \"c_retry\": return countTerm(term.term, stack);\n    }\n  };\n  return countGraph(owner.root.graphFunctionRef, []) === 1;\n}\nexport function projectQualificationConsumer(basis: QualificationNativeBasis, input: unknown, requireCurrent = false, resources?: QualificationResources) {\n  try {\n    if (!referenceDependencies(input, resources)) return null;\n    const events = readRuntimeEventsAtDurablePrefix(basis.predecessorPrefix as DurablePrefixCoordinate, { requireCurrent });\n    const owner = graphOwner(basis.predecessorPrefix as DurablePrefixCoordinate, basis.cCallRef, declarationsOf(input, resources),\n      { events, environments: new Map(), ...(resources === undefined ? {} : { resources }) });\n    if (owner === null || !QUALIFICATION_IMPLEMENTATION_REFS.includes(owner.call.implementationRef ?? \"\") ||\n        !sameFact(owner.input, input) || projectCCallCarrierPhaseAtPrefix(owner.prefix, owner.call)?.phase !== \"selected_no_evidence\") return null;\n    if (requireCurrent && owner.call.implementationRef === SELF && record(input) && record(input.qualification) &&\n        isQualificationProofResource(input.qualification.proof) && input.qualification.proof.executionSources !== undefined &&\n        (!record(input.basis) || executionSourceProofs(ownedProof(input.qualification.proof, resources),\n          input.basis as unknown as ExactCandidateQualification<\"basis\">, basis, true) === null)) return null;\n    if (owner.call.implementationRef === VERDICT && (!record(input) || input.slotRef !== owner.call.programLocusRef || !soleDeclaredReducer(owner))) return null;\n    if (owner.call.implementationRef === MALFORMED_ASSESS &&\n        (!isMalformedGtlAssessmentInput(input) || !malformedSubjectMatches(owner, input))) return null;\n    if (owner.call.implementationRef === RUNTIME_ASSESS &&\n        (!isNativeRuntimeAssessmentInput(input) || !malformedSubjectMatches(owner, input))) return null;\n    if (isQualificationAssessmentInput(input)) {\n      const slot = one(input.plan.slots.filter(s => s.slotRef === input.task.slotRef));\n      if (slot === null || slot.graphFunctionRef !== owner.call.graphFunctionRef || slot.programLocusRef !== owner.call.programLocusRef) return null;\n      const declaration = owner.publication.graphFunctions.find(g => g.name === owner.call.graphFunctionRef);\n      if (declaration?.declarations[\"abg.qualification_role_policy\"] !== hash(QUALIFICATION_ROLE_POLICY) ||\n          input.plan.ownerActorRef !== owner.execution.actorRef || input.plan.ownerAuthorityRef !== QUALIFICATION_ROLE_POLICY.authorityRef) return null;\n    }\n    return owner;\n  } catch { return null; }\n}\nfunction malformedSubjectMatches(owner: NonNullable<ReturnType<typeof graphOwner>>, input: { basis: ExactCandidateQualification<\"basis\"> }): boolean {\n i.packageName === row.packageName && i.packageVersion === row.packageVersion));\n  return install !== null && basis.productId === install.productId && basis.productVersion === install.packageVersion &&\n    basis.productContentDigest === install.productContentDigest && basis.artifact.digest === install.artifactDigest &&\n    basis.productManifest.digest === install.manifestDigest && basis.toolchain.digest === install.manifestDigest &&\n    same(basis.installedProduct, coord(install.installId, hash(install))) &&\n    same(basis.workspaceBinding, coord(owner.execution.workspaceBindingId, owner.execution.workspaceBindingDigest));\n}\n/** Reuse the native producer relation; the cited assessment is its exact typed\n * value, not a caller-authored assertion or an invented runtime Result ref. */\nexport function malformedGtlAssessmentHasNativeOwner(proof: QualificationProofResource, assessment: MalformedGtlAssessment, resources?: QualificationResources): boolean {\n  try {\n    proof = ownedProof(proof, resources);\n    if (!isMalformedGtlAssessment(assessment) || !isQualificationProofResource(proof) ||\n        !proofWithin({ ...proof, prefix: assessment.nativeBasis.predecessorPrefix }, { cCallRef: assessment.nativeBasis.cCallRef, predecessorPrefix: proof.prefix })) return false;\n    const source = nativeState(proof, assessment.nativeBasis.cCallRef);\n    if (source === null || source.state.cCall.callClass !== \"leaf\" || source.state.cCall.implementationRef !== MALFORMED_ASSESS ||\n        source.state.result.resultClass !== \"success\" || source.state.judgment.judgment !== \"advance\" ||\n        source.state.result.contractRef !== \"contract://abiogenesis/qualification/malformed-gtl-assessment@5\" ||\n        source.state.result.valueKind !== \"malformed_gtl_assessment\" ||\n        !same(source.state.result.value, assessment) || !sameFact(source.owner.input, assessment.input) ||\n        !malformedSubjectMatches(source.owner, assessment.input) ||\n        projectQualificationConsumer(assessment.nativeBasis, assessment.input) === null) return false;\n    const competitors = source.owner.events.filter(e => e.kind === \"c_call_opened\" && e.aggregateId !== source.state.cCall.cCallRef)\n      .map(e => nativeState(proof, e.aggregateId)).filter(s => s !== null && s.state.cCall.callClass === \"leaf\" &&\n        s.state.cCall.implementationRef === MALFORMED_ASSESS && s.state.cCall.programLocusRef === source.state.cCall.programLocusRef &&\n        s.owner.execution.invocationAdmissionRef === source.owner.execution.invocationAdmissionRef &&\n        s.state.result.resultClass === \"success\" && s.state.judgment.judgment === \"advance\");\n    return competitors.length === 0;\n  } catch { return false; }\n}\n/** Exclude only calls unable to affect the selected uniqueness relation.\n * Every potentially eligible successful producer still enters nativeState. */\nfunction eligibleCalls(events: readonly RuntimeEvent[], selected: GraphOwner, implementation?: string) {\n  const results = new Set(events.filter(e => e.kind === \"c_call_result_admitted\").map(e => e.aggregateId));\n  const implementations = implementation === undefined ? null : new Set(events.filter(e =>\n    e.kind === \"c_call_fibre_selected\" && record(e.payload) && e.payload.implementationRef === implementation).map(e => e.aggregateId));\n  return events.filter(e => e.kind === \"c_call_opened\" && record(e.payload) &&\n    e.payload.callClass === \"leaf\" && e.payload.programLocusRef === selected.call.programLocusRef &&\n    results.has(e.aggregateId) && (implementations === null || implementations.has(e.aggregateId)) &&\n    // All callers select uniqueness within this admitted invocation. Resolve\n    // that small owner fact before reconstructing an unrelated declaration.\n    e.basisId !== undefined && projectExactExecutionBasisAtPrefix(selected.prefix, e.basisId)?.invocationAdmissionRef ===\n      selected.execution.invocationAdmissionRef);\n}\ntype RuntimeCase = NativeRuntimeAssessmentInput[\"cases\"][number];\ntype RuntimeStep = Extract<RuntimeCase, { steps: unknown }>[\"steps\"][number];\n/** One producer relation shared by fibre and structural rows. Structural input\n * chaining is checked below against the actual preceding admitted value. */\nfunction nativeRuntimeStep(input: NativeRuntimeAssessmentInput, c: RuntimeCase, step: RuntimeStep,\n  proof: QualificationProofResource) {\n  const selected = producerForResult(proof, step.result);\n  if (selected === null || !malformedSubjectMatches(selected.owner, input) || selected.state.cCall.regime !== \"F_D\" ||\n    selected.state.cCall.callClass !== \"leaf\" || selected.state.cCall.programLocusRef !== step.slotRef ||\n    selected.state.cCall.implementationRef !== step.implementationRef || selected.owner.execution.programRef !== c.programRef ||\n    selected.owner.execution.invocationAdmissionRef !== c.invocationAdmissionRef ||\n    !same(coord(selected.owner.execution.graphFunctionRef, selected.owner.execution.graphFunctionDigest), c.graphFunction) ||\n    selected.state.result.resultClass !== \"success\" || selected.state.judgment.judgment !== \"advance\" ||\n    !same(coord(selected.state.result.resultRef, selected.state.result.resultDigest), step.result)) return null;\n  const evidence = selected.state.evidence;\n  if (evidence.length !== 1 || evidence[0]!.evidenceClass !== \"deterministic\" ||\n    evidence[0]!.implementationRef !== step.implementationRef || evidence[0]!.inputDigest !== step.inputDigest ||\n    evidence[0]!.outputDigest !== selected.state.result.valueDigest) return null;\n  const peers = eligibleCalls(selected.owner.events, selected.owner).map(e => nativeState(proof, e.aggregateId)).filter(s =>\n    s !== null && s.state.cCall.callClass === \"leaf\" && s.state.cCall.programLocusRef === step.slotRef &&\n    s.owner.execution.invocationAdmissionRef === c.invocationAdmissionRef && s.owner.execution.graphFunctionRef === c.graphFunction.ref &&\n    s.state.result.resultClass === \"success\" && s.state.judgment.judgment === \"advance\");\n  if (peers.length !== 1 || peers[0]!.state.cCall.cCallRef !== selected.state.cCall.cCallRef) return null;\n  return selected;\n}\n/** Native evidence assessment; no test runner, caller assertion or synthetic event supplies a case. */\nfunction nativeRuntimeCases(input: NativeRuntimeAssessmentInput, proof: QualificationProofResource): NativeRuntimeAssessment[\"cases\"] | null {\n  const cases: NativeRuntimeAssessment[\"cases\"][number][] = [];\n  for (const c of input.cases) {\n    const steps = \"structure\" in c ? c.steps : sources.at(-1)!;\n    if (steps[0]!.inputDigest !== first.owner.execution.rawInputDigest) return null;\n    if (\"structure\" in c) {\n      const node = one(first.owner.graph.template.nodes.filter(n => n.nodeRef === c.structure.nodeRef));\n      if (node === null || node.nodeKind !== \"c_locus\" || hash(node.term) !== c.structure.termDigest ||\n          node.term.kind !== (c.structure.kind === \"atomic_call\" ? \"c_of\" :\n            c.structure.kind === \"edge_program\" ? \"c_edge\" : \"c_compose\")) return null;\n      const terms = node.term.kind === \"c_compose\" ? node.term.terms : node.term.kind === \"c_edge\"\n        ? [node.term.transform, node.term.evaluate, node.term.consequence] : [node.term];\n      if (terms.length !== steps.length) return null;\n      for (const [i, term] of terms.entries()) {\n        const source = sources[i]!, call = source.state.cCall;\n        if (term.kind !== \"c_of\" || term.fibre !== \"F_D\" || (i === terms.length - 1 && !term.resultBearing) ||\n            (node.term.kind === \"c_edge\" && term.stageRole !== [\"transform\", \"evaluate\", \"consequence\"][i]) ||\n            term.requirement.kind !== \"executable_leaf_requirement\" || term.programLocusRef !== steps[i]!.slotRef ||\n            term.requirement.implementationBindingRef !== call.implementationBindingRef ||\n            call.graphCallId !== first.state.cCall.graphCallId || call.runId !== first.state.cCall.runId ||\n            call.frameId !== first.state.cCall.frameId || call.basisId !== first.state.cCall.basisId) return null;\n        if (i > 0) {\n          const previous = sources[i - 1]!;\n          const judgment = one(previous.owner.events.filter(e => e.kind === \"c_call_judged\" && e.aggregateId === previous.state.cCall.cCallRef));\n          if (judgment === null || judgment.admissionOrdinal >= source.owner.opened.admissionOrdinal ||\n              steps[i]!.inputDigest !== previous.state.result.valueDigest) return null;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    const observed = sources.map((source, i) => {\n      const step = steps[i]!;\n      return { cCallRef: source!.state.cCall.cCallRef, result: step.result,\n        evidence: source!.state.evidence.map(e => coord(e.evidenceRef, e.evidenceDigest)),\n        actualValueDigest: source!.state.result.valueDigest, matched: source!.state.result.valueDigest === step.expectedValueDigest,\n        substituteRefused: producerForResult(proof, coord(step.result.ref, step.substituteResultDigest)) === null };\n    });\n    const facts = nativeFacts(selected.owner.prefix), terminalKey = selected.state.cCall.graphCallId;\n    const runRows = selected.owner.events.filter(e => e.runId === selected.state.cCall.runId);\n    const knownTerminal = facts.terminals.get(terminalKey);\n    const retainedTerminal = knownTerminal !== undefined && runRows.length === knownTerminal.rows.length &&\n      runRows.every((e, i) => e === knownTerminal.rows[i]) ? knownTerminal : undefined;\n    const terminal = retainedTerminal?.terminal ?? DurablePrefixCoordinate, selected.state.cCall.runId);\n    if (terminal.kind !== \"abg_project_read_projection\" || !record(terminal.value) || !record(terminal.value.terminalResult) ||\n      !same(terminal.value.terminalResult.value, selected.state.result.value) ||\n      replay.kind !== \"abg_run_truth_projection\" || replay.runtimeStatus !== \"closed\") return null;\n    facts.terminals.set(terminalKey, { rows: runRows, terminal, replay });\n    const t = terminal.value.terminalResult;\n    if (!record(t.result) || typeof t.result.ref !== \"string\" || typeof t.result.digest !== \"string\") return null;\n    if (\"structure\" in c && producerForResult(proof, coord(t.result.ref, t.result.digest))?.state.cCall.cCallRef !== selected.state.cCall.cCallRef) substituteRefused: observed.every(s => s.substituteRefused),\n      ...(\"structure\" in c ? { steps: observed } : {}) });\n  }\n  return cases;\n}\nexport function projectNativeRuntimeAssessment(basis: QualificationNativeBasis, input: unknown, requireCurrent = false, resources?: QualificationResources): Readonly<NativeRuntimeAssessment> | null {\n  try {\n    if projectQualificationRulingForConsumer(basis: QualificationNativeBasis, input: unknown, requireCurrent = false, resources?: QualificationResources): Readonly<QualificationOwnerRuling> | null {\n  try }\n}\nfunction proofWithin(proof: QualificationProofResource, consumer: QualificationNativeBasis): boolean {\n  return same(proof.prefix.storeIdentity, consumer.predecessorPrefix.storeIdentity) &&\n    proof.prefix.eventLogRef === consumer.predecessorPrefix.eventLogRef &&\n    proof.prefix.prefixLength <= consumer.predecessorPrefix.prefixLength;\n}\nfunction nativeState(proof: QualificationProofResource, ref: string) {\n  const owner = graphOwner(proof.prefix as DurablePrefixCoordinate, ref, proof.declarations as readonly AbgHistoricalDeclarationProof[], executionProofFacts.get(proof));\n  const state = owner === null ? null : stateAt(owner);\n  if (owner === null || state === null) return null;\n  // These rows are consumed only after the existing QualificationProofResource, result: QualificationCoordinate) {\n  const events = proofEvents(proof);\n  const event = one(events.filter(e => e.kind === \"c_call_result_admitted\" && record(e.payload) &&\n    e.payload.resultRef === result.ref && e.payload.resultDigest === result.digest));\n  if (event === null) return null;\n  let current = nativeState(proof, event.aggregateId); const seen = new */\nexport function projectQualificationJudgment(proof: QualificationProofResource, plan: QualificationAssessmentPlan,\n  selection: Extract<QualificationEvidenceSelection, { kind: This validates a concrete\n * record chain under the declared trusted-desktop authority; labels alone fail. */\nexport function projectQualificationConstructionAuthors(proof: QualificationProofResource, plan: QualificationAssessmentPlan,\n  judgment: QualificationJudgment, resources?: QualificationResources): readonly string[] | null {\n  try {\n    proof = ownedProof(proof, resources);\n    const provenance = resolveQualificationAssessment({ kind: \"qualification_assessment_input\", schemaVersion: \"5.0.0\", task: judgment.task, plan: judgment.plan }, resources ?? executionProofFacts.get(proof)?.resources).task.provenance;\n    if (!same(provenance.subjectInventory, judgment.task.inventory)) return null;\n    if (provenance.kind === \"native_construction\") {\n      const actors: string[] = [], covered = new Set<string>();\n      if (!unique(provenance.producers.map(p => p.cCallRef))) return null;\n      for (const p of provenance.producers) {\n        const source = producerForResult(proof, p.result);\n        if (source === null || source.state.cCall.cCallRef !== p.cCallRef || source.state.result.resultClass !== \"success\" ||\n            source.state.judgment.judgment !== \"advance\" || source.state.cCall.implementationRef !== WORKSITE_CONSTRUCTION_IDS.candidateImplementationRef ||\n            !isWorksiteCandidateBundle(source.state.result.value)) return null;\n        // C1's actual admitted candidate owner establishes byte authorship,\n        // not C0 publication success. type ExactCandidateQualification, type QualificationOwnerRuling,\n  type QualificationRulingRequest,\n} from \"./qualification_contracts.js\";\nconst coordinate = (ref: string, digest: string) => ({ ref, digest });\nconst count = v.pipe(v.number(), v.integer(), v.minValue(0));\nconst text = v.pipe(v.string(), v.minLength(1));\nconst testReportRow = v.variant(\"type\", [\n  v.strictObject({ ordinal: count, type: v.literal(\"begin\"), format: v.literal(\"node-test-events-jsonl@1\"), nodeVersion: text }),\n  v.strictObject({ ordinal: count, type: v.literal(\"end\") }),\n  v.strictObject({ ordinal: count, type: v.literal(\"case\"), passed: v.boolean(), name: text,\n    file: v.nullable(text), nesting: count, testNumber: count, suite: v.boolean(),\n    skip: v.union([v.boolean(), v.string()]), todo: v.union([v.boolean(), v.string()]), error: v.nullable(jsonValueSchema) }),\n  v.strictObject({ ordinal: count, type: v.literal(\"summary\"), file: v.nullable(text),\n    counts: v.object({ tests: count, passed: count, failed: count, cancelled: count, skipped: count, todo: count, suites: count, topLevel: count }),\n    success: v.boolean(), durationMs: v.pipe(v.number(), v.minValue(0)) }),\n]);\nconst lintReport = v.strictObject({ kind: v.literal(\"qualification_syntax_lint\"), schemaVersion: v.literal(\"1\"),\n  passed: v.boolean(), files: v.array(v.strictObject({ path: text, kind: v.picklist([\"mjs\", \"json\"]),\n    disposition: v.picklist([\"passed\", \"failed\"]), diagnostic: v.nullable(text) })) });\nconst successfulCommand = (c: WorksiteCommandResult) => c.exitStatus === 0 && !c.timedOut &&\n  c.processSignal === null && c.signalSequence.length === 0 && c.terminationConfirmed;\nfunction streamText(c: WorksiteCommandResult, name: \"stdout\" | \"stderr\"): string {\n  const stream = c[name], bytes = Buffer.from(stream.payload, \"base64\");\n  if (bytes.toString(\"base64\") !== stream.payload || bytes.length !== stream.byteLength || sha256Bytes(bytes) !== stream.digest)\n    throw new TypeError(\"command stream bytes differ from their observation\");\n  return new TextDecoder(\"utf-8\", { fatal: true }).decode(bytes);\n}\n/** Parse only this frozen reporter protocol. Native admission remains at the\n * proof owner. Incomplete framing never supplies a zero-failure pass. */\nexport function parseQualificationTestReport(command: WorksiteCommandResult, files: readonly string[]): QualificationTestSummary {\n  const result: QualificationTestSummary = { commandId: command.commandId, streamDigest: command.stdout.digest,\n    format: \"node-test-events-jsonl@1\", disposition: \"blocked_incomplete\", diagnostics: [], tests: 0, passed: 0,\n    failed: 0, cancelled: 0, skipped: 0, todo: 0, suites: 0, files: [], cases: [], summaries: [], complete: false };\n  try {\n    const bytes = streamText(command, \"stdout\");\n    if (!bytes.endsWith(\"\\n\")) throw new TypeError(\"missing final newline\");\n    const rows = bytes.slice(0, -1).split(\"\\n\").map(line => v.parse(testReportRow, JSON.parse(line)));\n    if (rows.length < 3 || rows[0]!.type !== \"begin\" || rows.at(-1)!.type !== \"end\" ||\n        rows.some((r, i) => r.ordinal !== i || (r.type === \"begin\" && i !== 0) || (r.type === \"end\" && i !== rows.length - 1)))\n      throw new TypeError(\"missing, duplicate or unordered reporter framing\");\n    const summaries = rows.filter(r => r.type === \"summary\"), global = summaries.filter(r => r.file === null);\n    if (global.length !== 1 || rows.at(-2) !== global[0]) throw new TypeError(\"one final global summary is required\");\n    const summary = global[0]!, perFile = summaries.filter(r => r.file !== null), cases = rows.filter(r => r.type === \"case\"), leaves = cases.filter(c => !c.suite);\n    Object.assign(result, summary.counts);\n    // topLevel is runner metadata, not part of the published count contract.\n    delete (result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>).topLevel;\n    result.cases = cases as unknown as JsonValue[]; result.summaries = summaries as unknown as JsonValue[];\n    result.files = [...new Set(cases.flatMap(c => c.file === null ? [] : [c.file]))].sort();\n    result.complete = true;\n    if (!unique(files) || !same(result.files, [...files].sort()) || !unique(perFile.map(s => s.file!)) ||\n        !same(perFile.map(s => s.file!).sort(), [...files].sort())) result.diagnostics.push(\"test_file_population_mismatch\");\n    const skipped = leaves.filter(c => Boolean(c.skip)).length, todo = leaves.filter(c => !c.skip && Boolean(c.todo)).length;\n    const passed = leaves.filter(c => !c.skip && !c.todo && c.passed).length;\n    const unsuccessful = leaves.filter(c => !c.skip && !c.todo && !c.passed).length;\n    if (leaves.length !== result.tests || skipped !== result.skipped || todo !== result.todo || passed !== result.passed ||\n        unsuccessful !== result.failed + result.cancelled ||\n        result.suites !== cases.filter(c => c.suite).length ||\n        result.tests !== result.passed + result.failed + result.cancelled + result.skipped + result.todo ||\n        ([\"tests\", \"passed\", \"failed\", \"cancelled\", \"skipped\", \"todo\", \"suites\"] as const).some(key =>\n          perFile.reduce((sum, s) => sum + s.counts[key], 0) !== result[key]))\n      result.diagnostics.push(\"test_event_summary_mismatch\");\n    if (summary.success !== successfulCommand(command)) result.diagnostics.push(\"test_summary_process_mismatch\");\n    if (summaries.some(s => !s.success) || result.failed > 0 || result.cancelled > 0 || !successfulCommand(command)) {\n      result.disposition = \"failed\"; result.diagnostics.push(\"test_execution_failed\");\n    } else if (result.skipped > 0 || result.todo > 0 || result.tests === 0 || result.diagnostics.length > 0) {\n      result.diagnostics.push(\"test_selection_incomplete\");\n    } else result.disposition = \"passed\";\n  } catch (error) { result.diagnostics.push(\"test_report_incomplete: \" + String(error)); }\n  if (!successfulCommand(command)) { result.disposition = \"failed\"; if (!result.diagnostics.includes(\"test_execution_failed\")) result.diagnostics.push(\"test_execution_failed\"); }\n  return result;\n}\n/** Pure material construction from an already resolved producer. Callers cannot\n * supply this output to F11; its native join reconstructs it from the Result. */\nexport function constructQualificationVerificationMaterial(input: {\n  basis: ExactCandidateQualification<\"basis\">; inventory: QualificationSubjectInventory;\n  selection: QualificationVerificationSelection; execution: QualificationCoordinate; cCall: QualificationCoordinate;\n  observation: ObservedWorksiteCommandExecutionObservation;\n}): QualificationVerificationMaterial | null {\n  const { basis, inventory, selection, observation } = input;\n  try {\n    if (!isObservedWorksiteCommandExecutionObservation(observation)) return null;\n    const recipeBytes = Buffer.from(selection.recipe.contentBase64, \"base64\");\n    if (recipeBytes.toString(\"base64\") !== selection.recipe.contentBase64 || recipeBytes.length !== selection.recipe.byteCount ||\n        sha256Bytes(recipeBytes) !== selection.recipe.digest || \"qualification-inventory://abiogenesis/\") ||\n        inventory.members.filter(m => m.ref === selection.recipe.ref && m.path === selection.recipe.path &&\n          m.digest === selection.recipe.digest && m.byteCount === selection.recipe.byteCount).length !== 1) return null;\n    const recipe = v.parse(QUALIFICATION_VERIFICATION_RECIPE_SCHEMA, JSON.parse(new TextDecoder(\"utf-8\", { fatal: true }).decode(recipeBytes)));\n    const task = observation.task, diagnostics: string[] = [];\n    const selectedSources = recipe.sourceInputs.map(s => {\n      const members = inventory.members.filter(m => m.ref === s.memberRef);\n      if (members.length !== 1) throw new TypeError(\"recipe source is absent or ambiguous in qualification inventory\");\n      return { relativePath: s.relativePath, digest: members[0]!.digest, byteCount: members[0]!.byteCount };\n    });\n    const protectedInputs = [...selectedSources, ...recipe.auxiliaryInputs,\n      { relativePath: selection.recipePath, digest: selection.recipe.digest, byteCount: selection.recipe.byteCount }];\n    if (!unique(protectedInputs.map(s => s.relativePath)) || !unique(recipe.sourceInputs.map(s => s.memberRef)) ||\n        !same(protectedInputs.slice().sort((a, b) => a.relativePath.localeCompare(b.relativePath)),\n          task.protectedObservations.map(p => ({ relativePath: p.subject.relativePath,\n            digest: p.observation.state === \"file\" ? p.observation.fileDigest : null,\n            byteCount: p.observation.state === \"file\" ? p.observation.byteLength : null })).sort((a, b) => a.relativePath.localeCompare(b.relativePath))))\n      diagnostics.push(\"verification_source_inventory_mismatch\");\n    if (hash(task.commands) !== recipe.commandConfigurationDigest || hash(task.outcomePredicates) !== recipe.predicateConfigurationDigest ||\n        hash(task.allowedWriteTerritories) !== recipe.writeTerritoriesDigest ||\n        !same(recipe.commands.map(c => c.commandId), task.commands.map(c => c.commandId)) ||\n        !unique(recipe.commands.map(c => c.commandId)) || [\"build\", \"lint\", \"test\", \"compare\"].some(role => !recipe.commands.some(c => c.role === role)) ||\n        recipe.commands.filter(c => c.role === \"lint\").length !== 1 ||\n        !recipe.commands.some(c => c.commandId === recipe.lint.commandId && c.role === \"lint\") ||\n        !same(recipe.commands.filter(c => c.role === \"test\").map(c => c.commandId), recipe.tests.map(c => c.commandId)))\n      diagnostics.push(\"verification_recipe_configuration_mismatch\");\n    if (observation.productDelta.length > 0) diagnostics.push(\"verification_product_changed\");\n    const commandOutcomes = observation.commandResults.map(c => ({ commandId: c.commandId,\n      role: recipe.commands.find(r => r.commandId === c.commandId)?.role ?? \"setup\" as const,\n      observation: coordinate(c.observationRef, c.observationDigest), executable: c.executable, args: [...c.args],\n      relativeCwd: c.relativeCwd, environment: c.environment as unknown as JsonValue, timeoutMs:
